## Summary

| Directory               | Files | Contents                                |
|-------------------------|------:|-----------------------------------------|
| [config/](config)       |    39 | config dumps (obj, npc, loc, seq, vars) |
| [symbols/](symbols)     |    44 | ID <-> name tables                      |
| [worldmap/](worldmap)   |     1 | world map definitions                   |

| [interface/](interface) |   894 | interfaces (`.if3`: 894)                |
| [script/](script)       |  2833 | CS2 scripts                             |

Total: **3 812** files (excluding this README).

---

## Directory layout

```
578/
├── README.md
├── config/      dump.<type> - one file per config type
├── symbols/     *.sym - ID <-> name (+ constant/)
├── worldmap/    dump.wma
├── defaults/    *.defaults
├── interface/   interface_<ID>.if3
└── script/      [trigger,name].cs2
```

---

## config

Each `dump.*` file is a set of INI-style entries. An entry starts with a `[prefix_ID]` header (e.g. `[npc_0]`) followed by `key=value` pairs. Entries with only a header are unused IDs.

### Objects, NPCs, items

| File                                | Entry header  | Entries | ID range |   Size | Description                                                  |
|-------------------------------------|---------------|--------:|----------|-------:|--------------------------------------------------------------|
| [`dump.obj`](config/dump.obj)       | `[obj_ID]`    |  15 430 | 0-15431  | 2.9 MB | Objs - items                                                 |
| [`dump.npc`](config/dump.npc)       | `[npc_ID]`    |   9 408 | 0-9422   | 2.6 MB | NPCs - non-player characters and monsters                    |
| [`dump.loc`](config/dump.loc)       | `[loc_ID]`    |  47 899 | 0-47909  | 7.1 MB | Locs - world objects (trees, doors, rocks, buildings, etc.)  |
| [`dump.struct`](config/dump.struct) | `[struct_ID]` |     955 | 0-954    | 114 KB | Structs - data structures (parameter sets)                   |
| [`dump.param`](config/dump.param)   | `[param_ID]`  |     953 | 0-952    |  21 KB | Params - extra data attached to obj/npc/loc/struct           |
| [`dump.enum`](config/dump.enum)     | `[enum_ID]`   |   2 358 | 0-2576   | 497 KB | Enums - key -> value mappings used by scripts and interfaces |

| [`dump.bas`](config/dump.bas)                           | `[bas_ID]`      |   1 684 | 0-1683   |   99 KB | Basic animation sets - base animations (stand, walk, run) for players and NPCs |

## symbols

`.sym` files are lookup tables in the format `ID<TAB>name` (some have extra columns, e.g. a type). They translate IDs into symbolic names.

| [`category.sym`](symbols/category.sym)         |     87 | Categories                                          |

## Finding data

| Looking for                 | Where                                                                                                                        |
|-----------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Item with ID *N*            | [`config/dump.obj`](config/dump.obj) -> `[obj_N]`                                                                            |
| NPC with ID *N*             | [`config/dump.npc`](config/dump.npc) -> `[npc_N]`                                                                            |
| World object with ID *N*    | [`config/dump.loc`](config/dump.loc) -> `[loc_N]`                                                                            |
| Animation / GFX with ID *N* | [`config/dump.seq`](config/dump.seq) -> `[seq_N]` / [`config/dump.spot`](config/dump.spot) -> `[spotanim_N]`                 |
| Player variable / varbit    | [`config/dump.varp`](config/dump.varp) -> `[varplayer_N]` / [`config/dump.varbit`](config/dump.varbit) -> `[varplayerbit_N]` |
| Interface with ID *N*       | `interface/interface_N.if3` (see the [list](#interface))                                                                     |
| CS2 script                  | [list](#script) or [`symbols/clientscript.sym`](symbols/clientscript.sym)                                                    |
